[u5005 | 03:18:33.937-03:18:34.077 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5006 | 03:18:34.117-03:18:35.257 | Unknown] Yeah. That's why, that's why-
[u5007 | 03:18:35.257-03:18:35.877 | DM] So you didn't know that.
[u5008 | 03:18:36.197-03:18:38.477 | Wellby] He's getting out in, like, what, 10 days?
[u5009 | 03:18:38.477-03:18:38.497 | Unknown] 10 days.
[u5010 | 03:18:38.517-03:18:38.777 | Kenzo] 10 days. Yeah.
[u5011 | 03:18:38.777-03:18:39.077 | Wellby] Yeah.
[u5012 | 03:18:39.097-03:18:39.137 | Kenzo] Yes.
[u5013 | 03:18:39.137-03:18:39.697 | Wellby] So are we gonna have-
[u5014 | 03:18:39.797-03:18:40.817 | DM] Oh, shit.
[u5015 | 03:18:40.817-03:18:43.997 | Wellby] [laughs]
[u5016 | 03:18:44.017-03:18:44.017 | DM] Rogam's gonna be pissed.
[u5017 | 03:18:44.017-03:18:44.677 | Wellby] What's gonna be pissed?
[u5018 | 03:18:44.777-03:18:46.497 | DM] Rogam's gonna be pissed.
[u5019 | 03:18:46.537-03:18:47.077 | Kenzo] Pissed about what?
[u5020 | 03:18:47.157-03:18:48.877 | Delwath] Why is Rogam's gonna be pissed?
[u5021 | 03:18:49.497-03:18:51.597 | Delwath] What do you know about Rogam- Rogam'dros?
[u5022 | 03:18:52.597-03:18:53.037 | DM] I mean,
[u5023 | 03:18:54.177-03:18:57.637 | DM] I don't think she knows about me, and I wanna keep it that way.
[u5024 | 03:18:57.637-03:19:02.217 | DM] So I only get, like, little bits and pieces of rumors.
[u5025 | 03:19:03.977-03:19:04.657 | DM] Uh,
[u5026 | 03:19:06.697-03:19:07.817 | DM] but, like, you know
[u5027 | 03:19:08.977-03:19:10.817 | DM] Chaumut and Apollyon had a big fight, right?
[u5028 | 03:19:11.016-03:19:11.857 | Kenzo] Yeah, we know about that, yeah.
[u5029 | 03:19:11.917-03:19:12.776 | Unknown] Mm-hmm.
[u5030 | 03:19:12.776-03:19:14.997 | DM] You know Rogam'dros is Chaumut's daughter.
[u5031 | 03:19:15.677-03:19:15.776 | Unknown] What?
[u5032 | 03:19:17.157-03:19:18.297 | Kenzo] What? Did not know that.
[u5033 | 03:19:19.097-03:19:19.437 | DM] Some
[u5034 | 03:19:20.457-03:19:22.877 | DM] task she was appointed on this island.
[u5035 | 03:19:23.857-03:19:25.097 | DM] Something to guard, I think.
[u5036 | 03:19:25.897-03:19:26.957 | Unknown] Something to guard.
[u5037 | 03:19:29.597-03:19:31.497 | Unknown] Why do I think of Chaumut having sex?
[u5038 | 03:19:31.497-03:19:33.837 | Kenzo] [laughs]
[u5039 | 03:19:33.917-03:19:35.457 | DM] Who's to say dragons have-
[u5040 | 03:19:35.677-03:19:35.877 | Unknown] Could, could be-
[u5041 | 03:19:35.877-03:19:36.757 | DM] ... children the normal way?
[u5042 | 03:19:36.757-03:19:38.897 | Kenzo] Immaculate conception. Immaculate conception.
[u5043 | 03:19:38.897-03:19:40.277 | Unknown] Sure. Wow.
[u5044 | 03:19:40.397-03:19:40.977 | DM] Uh.
[u5045 | 03:19:40.997-03:19:41.897 | Unknown] What, what is she guarding?
[u5046 | 03:19:42.237-03:19:42.717 | DM] I don't know.
[u5047 | 03:19:43.337-03:19:44.117 | Unknown] It's not the jade.
[u5048 | 03:19:45.077-03:19:45.757 | DM] What, no.
[u5049 | 03:19:46.437-03:19:47.717 | Unknown] Because that's a more recent arrival.
[u5050 | 03:19:47.737-03:19:50.497 | Wellby] I mean, could it be the island itself
[u5051 | 03:19:51.277-03:19:51.777 | Wellby] somehow?
[u5052 | 03:19:52.317-03:19:56.257 | Kenzo] Could it be the divine forge that Apollyon used to make the scepter of power?
[u5053 | 03:19:56.397-03:19:57.657 | Wellby] Hmm, where is that, by the way?
[u5054 | 03:20:01.337-03:20:02.277 | DM] Somewhere west.
[u5055 | 03:20:03.657-03:20:04.197 | Kenzo] Uh.
[u5056 | 03:20:04.237-03:20:06.237 | Seeker] And, and how did you...
[u5057 | 03:20:06.257-03:20:07.117 | Unknown] Somewhere west on the island?
[u5058 | 03:20:07.377-03:20:08.917 | Kenzo] Yeah, that's the western part of the island. It's all weird.
[u5059 | 03:20:08.937-03:20:10.117 | Unknown] Yeah, the, the, the scary thing.
[u5060 | 03:20:11.977-03:20:13.977 | Seeker] How did you come to stay here, Dimitaur?
[u5061 | 03:20:14.637-03:20:15.477 | Kenzo] Oh, we don't know his name yet.
[u5062 | 03:20:15.797-03:20:15.937 | Unknown] Oh,
[u5063 | 03:20:17.457-03:20:21.057 | Unknown] I thought, I thought we confirmed the name earlier.
[u5064 | 03:20:21.057-03:20:21.917 | DM] That was out of game.
[u5065 | 03:20:21.957-03:20:22.217 | Unknown] Yeah.
[u5066 | 03:20:23.057-03:20:23.477 | DM] You can ask his name in game.
[u5067 | 03:20:23.477-03:20:25.137 | Kenzo] Yeah, yeah. Actually, yeah. What, what is your name?
[u5068 | 03:20:25.457-03:20:26.077 | DM] Dimitaur.
[u5069 | 03:20:26.677-03:20:26.997 | Kenzo] Huh.
[u5070 | 03:20:28.617-03:20:29.557 | Unknown] How did you come to stay here?
[u5071 | 03:20:30.177-03:20:30.377 | DM] Well,
[u5072 | 03:20:33.297-03:20:34.277 | DM] Rai never came back.
[u5073 | 03:20:35.877-03:20:36.277 | Kenzo] Back here?
[u5074 | 03:20:38.857-03:20:42.717 | DM] Back to where we were in Drankor keeping watch over Apollyon.
[u5075 | 03:20:43.357-03:20:44.517 | Kenzo] Hmm.
[u5076 | 03:20:44.537-03:20:45.897 | DM] I knew he had trained here.
[u5077 | 03:20:49.797-03:20:50.117 | DM] I thought,
[u5078 | 03:20:52.097-03:20:52.697 | DM] let's see.
[u5079 | 03:20:54.637-03:20:55.557 | Kenzo] How long have you been here now?
[u5080 | 03:20:57.317-03:20:59.889 | DM] Like...
[u5081 | 03:20:59.889-03:21:00.889 | DM] 35 years
[u5082 | 03:21:01.849-03:21:02.869 | Kenzo] Are you waiting for Rai here?
[u5083 | 03:21:06.409-03:21:08.569 | DM] Well, I didn't know so many of my people lived here.
[u5084 | 03:21:09.989-03:21:10.949 | DM] Now I'm kind of...
[u5085 | 03:21:11.829-03:21:12.629 | DM] I don't know what to do.
[u5086 | 03:21:15.369-03:21:15.469 | Kenzo] Hmm.
[u5087 | 03:21:16.769-03:21:19.089 | Seeker] What's your relationship like with, with the Dragonets?
[u5088 | 03:21:21.549-03:21:24.869 | DM] I'm pretty sure Ra'ghemdros would kill me if she knew I was here.
[u5089 | 03:21:25.629-03:21:25.889 | Unknown] Mm-hmm.
[u5090 | 03:21:26.509-03:21:32.369 | DM] So I don't really talk to them much, but some of them come find me secretly.
[u5091 | 03:21:33.809-03:21:36.909 | Seeker] Do you think you could enlist them to help us?
[u5092 | 03:21:37.669-03:21:38.649 | DM] Help you do what?
[u5093 | 03:21:40.229-03:21:42.709 | Seeker] We're trying to figure that out, but we need to get that jade.
[u5094 | 03:21:42.809-03:21:43.329 | DM] Ah,
[u5095 | 03:21:44.169-03:21:45.249 | DM] planners, I see.
[u5096 | 03:21:45.249-03:21:48.369 | Kenzo] [laughs]
[u5097 | 03:21:48.409-03:21:49.329 | Unknown] Planners, I see.
[u5098 | 03:21:49.349-03:21:51.049 | Kenzo] Does he say that with contempt or [laughs]
[u5099 | 03:21:51.149-03:21:52.289 | DM] A little bit with contempt.
[u5100 | 03:21:52.989-03:21:54.269 | Kenzo] I... We, we don't plan that much.
[u5101 | 03:21:54.509-03:21:55.349 | Kenzo] Don't worry. Um,
[u5102 | 03:21:57.129-03:21:57.689 | Kenzo] so you say-
[u5103 | 03:21:58.409-03:22:00.969 | DM] The important thing is to just not keep the plans you make.
[u5104 | 03:22:01.249-03:22:01.669 | Kenzo] Heh, we never do that.
[u5105 | 03:22:01.689-03:22:03.469 | Wellby] Yeah, we do. Yeah, yeah. Right.
[u5106 | 03:22:03.469-03:22:03.668 | Kenzo] So
[u5107 | 03:22:05.429-03:22:10.109 | Kenzo] the dragon, uh, is she, is she free?
[u5108 | 03:22:10.849-03:22:13.309 | Kenzo] We had a feeling she may also be captive.
[u5109 | 03:22:15.849-03:22:15.909 | DM] Eh.
[u5110 | 03:22:15.909-03:22:16.849 | Kenzo] Can she travel freely?
[u5111 | 03:22:19.069-03:22:19.769 | DM] You mean, like-
[u5112 | 03:22:20.189-03:22:21.009 | Kenzo] Can she leave her lair?
[u5113 | 03:22:21.669-03:22:22.369 | DM] Yeah.
[u5114 | 03:22:24.269-03:22:24.689 | DM] Is that...
[u5115 | 03:22:27.529-03:22:29.489 | DM] Where did you get this idea from?
[u5116 | 03:22:29.629-03:22:29.709 | Kenzo] Uh.
[u5117 | 03:22:31.029-03:22:33.989 | DM] I mean, do you know many dragons that don't leave their lairs?
[u5118 | 03:22:34.569-03:22:36.449 | Delwath] She hasn't seen too much
[u5119 | 03:22:37.329-03:22:38.909 | Delwath] according to the Dragonets down there.
[u5120 | 03:22:39.529-03:22:40.829 | DM] Well, she has some task.
[u5121 | 03:22:41.689-03:22:42.989 | DM] Maybe it's too important.
[u5122 | 03:22:42.989-03:22:43.489 | DM] I don't know.
[u5123 | 03:22:45.649-03:22:47.849 | Seeker] An important task underground?
[u5124 | 03:22:51.869-03:22:54.729 | DM] I'm not here to solve your problems, guys.
[u5125 | 03:22:54.729-03:22:56.489 | Wellby] [laughs]
[u5126 | 03:22:56.489-03:22:57.569 | Unknown] Well, what can we do to help you?
[u5127 | 03:22:57.569-03:23:04.789 | DM] [coughs] Wait, you want to help me?
[u5128 | 03:23:04.789-03:23:05.069 | Unknown] Yeah.
[u5129 | 03:23:08.869-03:23:09.369 | DM] Well, um,
[u5130 | 03:23:11.949-03:23:14.409 | DM] something more interesting to eat would be nice.
[u5131 | 03:23:15.829-03:23:16.889 | Kenzo] We can help you with that.
[u5132 | 03:23:16.889-03:23:18.389 | Wellby] We've got these berries. They're pretty good.
[u5133 | 03:23:20.229-03:23:21.949 | DM] Oh, well, fruit's easy.
[u5134 | 03:23:22.849-03:23:24.029 | DM] My circles make it.
[u5135 | 03:23:24.729-03:23:26.409 | Delwath] Hmm. I'll send the...
[u5136 | 03:23:26.409-03:23:28.389 | Delwath] my lynxes out to go collect some food.
[u5137 | 03:23:29.049-03:23:30.449 | Kenzo] I can make you a meal, Dimitar.
[u5138 | 03:23:30.449-03:23:32.929 | Seeker] I suppose this runic food gets a little old
[u5139 | 03:23:33.829-03:23:33.909 | Unknown] day in and day out.
[u5140 | 03:23:33.929-03:23:36.169 | DM] Yeah, it's like 12 menu choices.
[u5141 | 03:23:37.049-03:23:37.469 | Kenzo] That's...
[u5142 | 03:23:38.089-03:23:39.349 | DM] You gotta go out there. There's...
[u5143 | 03:23:39.369-03:23:42.369 | DM] The biggest thing is none of the ones up here are seafood.
[u5144 | 03:23:43.469-03:23:43.869 | Kenzo] Ah.
[u5145 | 03:23:43.989-03:23:48.449 | Seeker] Of course, you like a little seafood once in a while, but it's hard to get down there 'cause of all the other Dragonets, so...
[u5146 | 03:23:49.509-03:23:51.269 | DM] Also, you know, at my age,
[u5147 | 03:23:52.029-03:23:54.549 | DM] I'm not as agile as I once was in the air.
[u5148 | 03:23:54.829-03:23:55.369 | Unknown] Hmm.
[u5149 | 03:23:55.369-03:23:56.089 | Kenzo] Hmm. Yeah.
[u5150 | 03:23:56.149-03:23:57.249 | Unknown] Of course. Of course.
[u5151 | 03:23:57.429-03:23:57.549 | Kenzo] Yeah. Hmm.
[u5152 | 03:23:59.769-03:24:01.869 | Kenzo] Uh, so just so you know,
[u5153 | 03:24:03.449-03:24:04.669 | Kenzo] this is our plan.
[u5154 | 03:24:04.669-03:24:07.569 | Kenzo] I know you're not, you know, but just curious your thoughts on it.
[u5155 | 03:24:07.569-03:24:10.429 | Kenzo] We, uh, want to get that last piece of jade.
[u5156 | 03:24:12.049-03:24:17.329 | Kenzo] Um, I think when we put those pieces together, um, you know, I mentioned I got a...
[u5157 | 03:24:17.329-03:24:21.629 | Kenzo] been getting a vision of, of Rai basically every time we find one of these pieces of jade.
[u5158 | 03:24:23.469-03:24:23.789 | Kenzo] Um,
[u5159 | 03:24:25.169-03:24:25.609 | Kenzo] we
[u5160 | 03:24:26.629-03:24:30.489 | Kenzo] suspect at least that once we get the final piece together,
[u5161 | 03:24:31.889-03:24:36.189 | Kenzo] we're gonna get a pretty important piece of information about where Apollyon is,
[u5162 | 03:24:37.749-03:24:39.629 | Kenzo] um, storing himself,
[u5163 | 03:24:41.109-03:24:43.249 | Kenzo] and probably in Drankor someplace.
[u5164 | 03:24:43.249-03:24:43.829 | DM] Where-
[u5165 | 03:24:43.869-03:24:44.569 | Kenzo] Probably gonna have to go in.
[u5166 | 03:24:44.569-03:24:46.109 | DM] Wait, you don't know that already?
[u5167 | 03:24:46.889-03:24:47.129 | Kenzo] Do you know?
[u5168 | 03:24:47.149-03:24:48.589 | DM] You haven't, like, scouted?
[u5169 | 03:24:48.589-03:24:49.689 | Kenzo] We haven't been to Drankor yet, no.
[u5170 | 03:24:50.209-03:24:51.809 | DM] His tomb is pretty obvious.
[u5171 | 03:24:51.949-03:24:52.089 | Kenzo] Yeah?
[u5172 | 03:24:53.069-03:24:55.069 | Delwath] But his tomb is not where his, uh-
[u5173 | 03:24:55.569-03:24:56.029 | Wellby] Phylactery is
[u5174 | 03:24:56.029-03:24:56.809 | Unknown] ... phylactery is.
[u5175 | 03:24:57.969-03:24:58.429 | Unknown] That would be pretty tough.
[u5176 | 03:24:58.429-03:24:59.769 | DM] Oh, you want to kill him,
[u5177 | 03:25:01.149-03:25:02.189 | DM] not just lock him up?
[u5178 | 03:25:03.429-03:25:03.749 | Unknown] If you can.
[u5179 | 03:25:03.789-03:25:04.989 | Wellby] Yeah, how would we lock him up?
[u5180 | 03:25:05.569-03:25:07.049 | DM] I don't know. Retie the knots.
[u5181 | 03:25:07.069-03:25:07.889 | DM] Aren't you magic?
[u5182 | 03:25:08.409-03:25:09.329 | Kenzo] Eh, not that way.
[u5183 | 03:25:09.749-03:25:09.849 | Wellby] Yeah, no.
[u5184 | 03:25:09.849-03:25:11.129 | Kenzo] I mean, maybe Rai could help us.
[u5185 | 03:25:14.129-03:25:15.908 | DM] Oh, well, that's much harder.
[u5186 | 03:25:16.489-03:25:17.209 | Kenzo] Why is that much harder?
[u5187 | 03:25:17.648-03:25:19.449 | DM] Well, I don't know where his phylactery is.
[u5188 | 03:25:19.929-03:25:23.069 | Kenzo] I... But, well, that's why we get the last piece of jade and then we figure out where the phylactery is.
[u5189 | 03:25:23.069-03:25:24.669 | Unknown] [laughs] Have you been listening to our story?
[u5190 | 03:25:24.749-03:25:26.789 | DM] Oh, you think Rai knows where his phylactery is?
[u5191 | 03:25:27.329-03:25:28.609 | DM] Oh, maybe he does, actually.
[u5192 | 03:25:30.889-03:25:31.129 | DM] Hmm.
[u5193 | 03:25:32.649-03:25:33.449 | DM] Interesting.
[u5194 | 03:25:33.449-03:25:34.549 | Kenzo] Can you help us find Rai?
[u5195 | 03:25:36.309-03:25:36.869 | DM] I looked.
[u5196 | 03:25:38.089-03:25:39.349 | Kenzo] Yeah, in Drankor, huh? Couldn't find him.
[u5197 | 03:25:39.349-03:25:40.049 | DM] He never came back.
[u5198 | 03:25:40.669-03:25:40.949 | Wellby] Hmm.
[u5199 | 03:25:42.049-03:25:43.149 | DM] He went off with the monks.
[u5200 | 03:25:46.049-03:25:46.149 | Wellby] Oh, the monks.
[u5201 | 03:25:46.149-03:25:48.629 | DM] Wait, that was your friends who convinced him to do that?
[u5202 | 03:25:50.389-03:25:52.389 | Kenzo] I think Rai was the one that was convincing them.
[u5203 | 03:25:53.129-03:25:54.129 | DM] Oh, that's possible.
[u5204 | 03:25:54.209-03:25:54.369 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5205 | 03:25:54.369-03:25:55.369 | Wellby] [laughs]
[u5206 | 03:25:55.409-03:25:56.849 | Kenzo] The other way around with the convincing.
[u5207 | 03:25:57.649-03:26:00.529 | DM] I know he did. Like, they had a few little successes.
[u5208 | 03:26:01.409-03:26:02.249 | Kenzo] Successes doing what?
[u5209 | 03:26:03.029-03:26:04.609 | DM] Cleaning up the Seeds of Chaos.
[u5210 | 03:26:05.009-03:26:05.289 | Unknown] Right.
[u5211 | 03:26:05.849-03:26:06.129 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5212 | 03:26:07.169-03:26:08.309 | DM] Then they went for the big one.
[u5213 | 03:26:09.949-03:26:10.929 | DM] I thought it was a mistake.
[u5214 | 03:26:12.109-03:26:13.169 | Kenzo] Does Cha'mut have other children?
[u5215 | 03:26:13.589-03:26:14.249 | Unknown] If only he'd listened.
[u5216 | 03:26:15.409-03:26:16.829 | DM] Yeah, if only Rai... Rai never...
[u5217 | 03:26:17.689-03:26:18.749 | DM] He didn't listen to me.
[u5218 | 03:26:19.809-03:26:20.249 | DM] Damn it.
[u5219 | 03:26:21.009-03:26:22.909 | Kenzo] What, what, what, what, what was your advice to Rai?
[u5220 | 03:26:22.929-03:26:23.749 | Kenzo] What were you trying to get him to do?
[u5221 | 03:26:23.809-03:26:29.469 | DM] Don't go to the gi- the biggest, giantest Seed of Chaos and try and fix it in Isingue.
[u5222 | 03:26:29.469-03:26:31.189 | DM] That seems like a stupid thing to do.
[u5223 | 03:26:33.629-03:26:35.869 | Seeker] That's how all those, those monks were lost, presumably.
[u5224 | 03:26:35.889-03:26:35.909 | DM] Mm.
[u5225 | 03:26:37.029-03:26:40.729 | Delwath] Remember, that's the acidic ooze that we flew over.
[u5226 | 03:26:40.729-03:26:41.089 | Kenzo] What is?
[u5227 | 03:26:42.369-03:26:42.369 | DM] Oh.
[u5228 | 03:26:45.709-03:26:45.749 | Kenzo] Oh.
[u5229 | 03:26:45.749-03:26:47.129 | Wellby] Yeah. No, I think you were right.
[u5230 | 03:26:47.129-03:26:48.829 | Unknown] [laughs] Dimitar's-
[u5231 | 03:26:48.889-03:26:49.949 | DM] This one's smart
[u5232 | 03:26:49.949-03:26:51.949 | Seeker] ... Dimitar's got some insights into how the world works.
[u5233 | 03:26:52.309-03:26:55.149 | Delwath] But maybe after we kill Apollyon we should go figure it out.
[u5234 | 03:26:56.589-03:26:58.689 | Seeker] Well, we should see what Dimitar says about that.
[u5235 | 03:26:58.729-03:27:00.689 | Kenzo] Yeah. Wait, but, yeah, but, but what were you trying to tell Rai?
[u5236 | 03:27:00.689-03:27:02.909 | Unknown] So your, your, your advice to Rai was-
[u5237 | 03:27:03.169-03:27:04.389 | Unknown] It did hurt our ship after all
[u5238 | 03:27:04.389-03:27:06.329 | DM] ... don't go. It's the biggest place of all.
[u5239 | 03:27:06.349-03:27:07.389 | Unknown] We gotta take revenge
[u5240 | 03:27:09.245-03:27:09.585 | Unknown] It's true.
[u5241 | 03:27:09.645-03:27:11.205 | DM] But he was pretty sure he could do it.
[u5242 | 03:27:11.645-03:27:11.865 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5243 | 03:27:13.325-03:27:13.525 | Unknown] Huh.
[u5244 | 03:27:15.085-03:27:15.405 | Kenzo] Um,
[u5245 | 03:27:16.625-03:27:17.485 | Kenzo] Shemut has other children?
[u5246 | 03:27:18.345-03:27:18.805 | DM] I don't know.
[u5247 | 03:27:19.505-03:27:20.645 | Kenzo] How do you know this is Shemut's daughter?
[u5248 | 03:27:22.685-03:27:24.525 | Unknown] You haven't heard her brag?
[u5249 | 03:27:25.525-03:27:25.965 | Kenzo] What's brag?
[u5250 | 03:27:26.985-03:27:28.385 | Unknown] When did you talk to her?
[u5251 | 03:27:29.325-03:27:31.345 | DM] I didn't talk to her. You didn't ask it.
[u5252 | 03:27:31.985-03:27:33.565 | DM] You just never bothered to ask.
[u5253 | 03:27:33.585-03:27:33.625 | DM] Like-
[u5254 | 03:27:33.685-03:27:34.125 | Kenzo] Ask who?
[u5255 | 03:27:34.505-03:27:37.205 | DM] Any of the stupid dragonets flying around.
[u5256 | 03:27:38.205-03:27:39.865 | DM] I am... He's, like, imitating somebody.
[u5257 | 03:27:40.685-03:27:43.365 | DM] I am Ra'ghemdros, daughter of Shemut.
[u5258 | 03:27:43.385-03:27:50.025 | DM] I am the Light Stealer and the Final Shadow, the Devourer of Drankor, known as the Eternal Night.
[u5259 | 03:27:50.505-03:27:51.965 | DM] She's not shy about it.
[u5260 | 03:27:52.165-03:27:53.105 | Kenzo] She's pretty boastful, huh?
[u5261 | 03:27:53.545-03:27:54.705 | Unknown] Those are some good names though.
[u5262 | 03:27:55.285-03:27:55.545 | Kenzo] Yeah?
[u5263 | 03:27:56.265-03:27:58.385 | Unknown] Uh, we haven't seen the ending though.
[u5264 | 03:27:58.485-03:28:00.205 | Kenzo] Yeah, we have. We just, we just haven't been there that long.
[u5265 | 03:28:00.205-03:28:04.225 | Kenzo] We've only been here for half a day. So, so okay, so she's Shemut's daughter.
[u5266 | 03:28:04.225-03:28:06.205 | Kenzo] She's here on some task that Shemut
[u5267 | 03:28:07.445-03:28:08.185 | Kenzo] has put her to.
[u5268 | 03:28:08.905-03:28:09.045 | Unknown] Right.
[u5269 | 03:28:09.045-03:28:09.585 | Kenzo] Nominally.
[u5270 | 03:28:09.665-03:28:09.825 | DM] Yep.
[u5271 | 03:28:11.365-03:28:13.945 | Kenzo] And you don't know what that task is.
[u5272 | 03:28:14.965-03:28:17.185 | Delwath] Hey, did the hobgoblin wizard who's in the...
[u5273 | 03:28:17.185-03:28:18.365 | Unknown] She was against Shemut, right?
[u5274 | 03:28:18.465-03:28:19.365 | DM] Yeah.
[u5275 | 03:28:19.365-03:28:21.885 | Delwath] You know who might be an ally killing Shemut's daughter?
[u5276 | 03:28:24.185-03:28:26.105 | Seeker] Is the hobgoblin wi- wizard we have trapped in the mirror?
[u5277 | 03:28:26.185-03:28:26.425 | Unknown] Yeah.
[u5278 | 03:28:26.425-03:28:26.645 | DM] Yeah.
[u5279 | 03:28:26.805-03:28:29.165 | Wellby] She's not gonna go back in the mirror again after what's happened.
[u5280 | 03:28:29.225-03:28:30.085 | DM] No, of course not.
[u5281 | 03:28:30.445-03:28:32.765 | Seeker] No, her... What she gets in re- in return is...
[u5282 | 03:28:33.345-03:28:34.625 | Unknown] Well, no, we can't give her the island.
[u5283 | 03:28:36.805-03:28:39.285 | Wellby] We can't exactly stop her from taking [laughs].
[u5284 | 03:28:39.285-03:28:40.825 | Delwath] We could, we could try to kill her though.
[u5285 | 03:28:42.305-03:28:42.445 | Unknown] Hm.
[u5286 | 03:28:42.445-03:28:43.225 | DM] Double cross her.
[u5287 | 03:28:44.285-03:28:44.925 | DM] Maybe a bad idea.
[u5288 | 03:28:45.005-03:28:46.205 | Unknown] I, yeah, that's-
[u5289 | 03:28:46.245-03:28:47.505 | DM] But, like, there's something here.
[u5290 | 03:28:48.345-03:28:48.525 | Kenzo] Okay.
[u5291 | 03:28:48.525-03:28:49.785 | Unknown] But she was an ally of Shemut?
[u5292 | 03:28:49.905-03:28:50.285 | DM] Enemy.
[u5293 | 03:28:50.505-03:28:50.505 | Kenzo] Enemy.
[u5294 | 03:28:50.525-03:28:52.025 | DM] But she was trapped by...
[u5295 | 03:28:52.025-03:28:56.185 | DM] She tried to trap Shemut in the mirror, and he was like, "No, no, no, you go in the mirror."
[u5296 | 03:28:56.225-03:28:56.345 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5297 | 03:28:56.405-03:28:58.805 | Wellby] Oh, maybe we could s- maybe she knows about Ra'ghemdros.
[u5298 | 03:28:59.505-03:29:00.425 | DM] We could talk to her.
[u5299 | 03:29:01.125-03:29:01.905 | Unknown] That'd be a great person to talk to.
[u5300 | 03:29:01.965-03:29:02.645 | Unknown] I think it's a good idea.
[u5301 | 03:29:03.105-03:29:03.125 | Unknown] Yeah.
[u5302 | 03:29:03.145-03:29:03.785 | Unknown] I think it's a good idea.
[u5303 | 03:29:05.185-03:29:05.765 | Kenzo] Yeah, uh-
[u5304 | 03:29:05.865-03:29:09.545 | Delwath] She might help us just to see Ra'ghemdros die, even in not being freed.
[u5305 | 03:29:09.665-03:29:17.565 | Wellby] But also, what if she's been biding her time this entire time, and, like, could have left the prison at any moment, and now we take her out and talk to her and-
[u5306 | 03:29:18.245-03:29:19.485 | Unknown] We're not taking her out to talk to her.
[u5307 | 03:29:19.525-03:29:20.585 | Unknown] We're talking to her in the mirror.
[u5308 | 03:29:20.585-03:29:20.645 | DM] No, we can talk to her in the mirror.
[u5309 | 03:29:20.685-03:29:21.705 | Unknown] Hm.
[u5310 | 03:29:21.785-03:29:22.885 | DM] I, I think we should talk to her.
[u5311 | 03:29:22.925-03:29:25.745 | Kenzo] Hold on, hold on. But let's just... We're, th- we're talking to Dimitaur now, right?
[u5312 | 03:29:25.765-03:29:27.605 | Kenzo] So what else do we do- want to learn from Dimitaur?
[u5313 | 03:29:31.205-03:29:32.605 | Kenzo] Can you help us find Rai
[u5314 | 03:29:34.045-03:29:42.705 | Kenzo] now? 'Cause we're going to Drankor next after this, almost certainly.
[u5315 | 03:29:44.585-03:29:46.585 | DM] I do not wanna go back to Drankor.
[u5316 | 03:29:46.585-03:29:47.625 | DM] [coughs]
[u5317 | 03:29:47.625-03:29:49.325 | Delwath] But you know it pretty well.
[u5318 | 03:29:52.065-03:29:52.585 | DM] I do.
[u5319 | 03:29:53.385-03:29:53.625 | Unknown] Okay.
[u5320 | 03:29:54.025-03:29:55.605 | Kenzo] One favor.
[u5321 | 03:29:55.605-03:29:56.985 | Kenzo] Maybe you can help us with a map.
[u5322 | 03:29:57.005-03:29:59.285 | Delwath] Hm. I was gonna say, can we spend, like, two hours
[u5323 | 03:30:00.625-03:30:03.205 | Delwath] digging through everything he knows about Drankor?
[u5324 | 03:30:03.225-03:30:06.005 | DM] You can, but you can't hear it now because I haven't made it up.
[u5325 | 03:30:06.005-03:30:07.325 | Unknown] Yes, I know. That's what I was just gonna say.
[u5326 | 03:30:07.465-03:30:07.905 | Kenzo] That's fine.
[u5327 | 03:30:09.105-03:30:09.665 | DM] Um-
[u5328 | 03:30:10.425-03:30:15.685 | Delwath] I mean, would he... What, why don't we just, like, c- c- could you tell us everything you know?
[u5329 | 03:30:17.825-03:30:19.305 | DM] Yeah, I could tell you some stuff.
[u5330 | 03:30:19.945-03:30:21.185 | Kenzo] Look, here's the basics.
[u5331 | 03:30:22.025-03:30:23.745 | Kenzo] We get the last piece of jade.
[u5332 | 03:30:23.745-03:30:26.105 | Kenzo] Hopefully, that tells us where the phylactery is.
[u5333 | 03:30:26.105-03:30:30.165 | Kenzo] Almost certainly it's gonna be in Drankor, and then we're gonna go and try and destroy the phylactery.
[u5334 | 03:30:30.165-03:30:32.425 | Kenzo] We expect it's gonna be really hard going there.
[u5335 | 03:30:32.425-03:30:36.505 | Kenzo] Is Drankor now, like, is it, like, some haunted, like, fucked up place that's gonna, like, try to kill us at every turn?
[u5336 | 03:30:39.485-03:30:41.685 | DM] It wasn't really like that when Rai was there.
[u5337 | 03:30:42.965-03:30:44.365 | DM] I bet it's gotten a lot worse.
[u5338 | 03:30:44.885-03:30:45.385 | Kenzo] Since when?
[u5339 | 03:30:45.725-03:30:46.365 | Unknown] Since Rai left.
[u5340 | 03:30:46.865-03:30:47.505 | DM] Since Rai left.
[u5341 | 03:30:47.545-03:30:48.365 | Kenzo] Like 39 years ago?
[u5342 | 03:30:48.565-03:30:48.745 | Unknown] Yeah.
[u5343 | 03:30:48.765-03:30:48.765 | DM] Yeah.
[u5344 | 03:30:48.825-03:30:49.465 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5345 | 03:30:49.605-03:30:50.945 | DM] I couldn't stay much,
[u5346 | 03:30:51.945-03:30:54.265 | DM] like, only a few months.
[u5347 | 03:30:54.805-03:31:05.005 | DM] I only stayed there a few months waiting for him, and it got dark and scary, and there was this, like, aura of fear that radiated out through the whole place coming
[u5348 | 03:31:05.005-03:31:09.425 | DM] from Apollyon's tomb. And every morning, I woke up wanting to run away, and eventually I did.
[u5349 | 03:31:09.685-03:31:09.905 | Kenzo] Hm.
[u5350 | 03:31:09.925-03:31:11.665 | DM] And I'm not sure I could go back.
[u5351 | 03:31:12.185-03:31:13.645 | Kenzo] Yeah, it doesn't sound like a good place.
[u5352 | 03:31:15.645-03:31:15.905 | Kenzo] Um-
[u5353 | 03:31:18.305-03:31:20.325 | Delwath] Collect immunity to fear artifacts.
[u5354 | 03:31:20.925-03:31:21.965 | Kenzo] Or a feast.
[u5355 | 03:31:23.085-03:31:23.745 | Unknown] I think one of the binding-
[u5356 | 03:31:23.765-03:31:24.225 | Unknown] Or Rizwin
[u5357 | 03:31:24.225-03:31:24.625 | Unknown] ... things can
[u5358 | 03:31:25.625-03:31:27.425 | Unknown] be Rizwin. Or Rizwin.
[u5359 | 03:31:27.465-03:31:29.024 | Seeker] I wonder how Rizwin's doing. Oh, well.
[u5360 | 03:31:29.065-03:31:30.005 | Kenzo] So, so-
[u5361 | 03:31:30.024-03:31:30.405 | Unknown] Who knows?
[u5362 | 03:31:31.125-03:31:35.385 | Kenzo] Okay. Anyways, yeah, we appreciate any insights you have 'cause I think we're gonna have to go there.
[u5363 | 03:31:35.385-03:31:37.545 | Kenzo] It sounds pretty scary, but we are gonna try to go there.
[u5364 | 03:31:38.065-03:31:42.705 | Kenzo] And when we go there, if Rai is still there, if, do you have any tips on if we wanna try to find him?
[u5365 | 03:31:46.645-03:31:47.765 | Kenzo] Would you want, would you want his hand back?
[u5366 | 03:31:49.785-03:31:50.205 | DM] I mean,
[u5367 | 03:31:52.545-03:31:55.465 | DM] if I took your hand from you and then showed up, would you want it back?
[u5368 | 03:31:56.985-03:31:57.745 | Kenzo] How do we give it back to him?
[u5369 | 03:31:58.345-03:32:01.745 | DM] I don't know. Like, maybe you could take it and hand it to him.
[u5370 | 03:32:02.385-03:32:03.185 | Unknown] Hand it to him?
[u5371 | 03:32:03.225-03:32:03.725 | Kenzo] Here he is.
[u5372 | 03:32:04.405-03:32:05.365 | DM] This one's got it.
[u5373 | 03:32:05.645-03:32:06.045 | Unknown] Nice.
[u5374 | 03:32:06.445-03:32:07.785 | Kenzo] So, so, so-
[u5375 | 03:32:07.845-03:32:08.225 | Unknown] Nice
[u5376 | 03:32:08.225-03:32:10.605 | Kenzo] ... but yeah, but do you, any, any, any ideas how to find him?
[u5377 | 03:32:12.365-03:32:13.785 | Unknown] Do, do we even know he's findable?
[u5378 | 03:32:13.785-03:32:14.465 | Unknown] I mean, we don't actually-
[u5379 | 03:32:14.565-03:32:15.745 | Kenzo] We're asking. I mean, I don't know.
[u5380 | 03:32:15.805-03:32:17.145 | Unknown] Didn't Dimitaur say that-
[u5381 | 03:32:17.165-03:32:19.585 | Kenzo] Would we even know if he was dead? No, he said he d- can't ever die.
[u5382 | 03:32:19.585-03:32:20.445 | Kenzo] The part is something, somewhere-
[u5383 | 03:32:20.485-03:32:25.125 | Delwath] I mean, he said he couldn't find him and he went looking, and he also said he doesn't think Rai can ever die.
[u5384 | 03:32:25.305-03:32:28.345 | DM] But he wasn't in... Dimitaur could not find him in Drankor.
[u5385 | 03:32:28.405-03:32:28.585 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5386 | 03:32:29.105-03:32:32.865 | DM] Maybe he was, like, trapped for a while and then came back to Drankor somehow.
[u5387 | 03:32:32.885-03:32:42.465 | Kenzo] But what, what I'm trying to say is when you're, when you're looking for some kind of weird, you know, immortal sorcerer or whatever, like, does he respond to anything or, like, is there anything that-
[u5388 | 03:32:42.485-03:32:44.805 | DM] I don't know. Send your, like, spirit dog after him.
[u5389 | 03:32:45.825-03:32:47.105 | Kenzo] Is that what you try to do when you find him?
[u5390 | 03:32:47.125-03:32:47.985 | Kenzo] Like, what do you do when you try to find him?
[u5391 | 03:32:47.985-03:32:50.425 | Delwath] Actually, that's not a bad idea. Why didn't we think of that?
[u5392 | 03:32:50.425-03:32:52.445 | Unknown] [laughs]
[u5393 | 03:32:52.465-03:32:54.665 | DM] I don't know. You're just not as smart as he, I guess.
[u5394 | 03:32:54.785-03:32:58.605 | Kenzo] I mean, we're clearly not, but, like, any, any other tips, any other advice for, for finding him?
[u5395 | 03:33:01.305-03:33:01.785 | DM] I mean,
[u5396 | 03:33:03.085-03:33:05.485 | DM] the last place I know he was was Isingay.
[u5397 | 03:33:06.805-03:33:07.385 | Kenzo] Isingay?
[u5398 | 03:33:07.985-03:33:09.105 | Unknown] That's where that acidic ooze is
[u5399 | 03:33:10.025-03:33:10.305 | Kenzo] Huh?
[u5400 | 03:33:10.385-03:33:11.765 | Unknown] When we're all... The whole...
[u5401 | 03:33:11.785-03:33:11.905 | Unknown] It was like-
[u5402 | 03:33:11.945-03:33:15.625 | Delwath] Remember we flew, we flew over Isingue in Vincharna.
[u5403 | 03:33:15.685-03:33:15.845 | Unknown] Yeah.
[u5404 | 03:33:15.885-03:33:19.825 | Delwath] And we went down to investigate 'cause it looked cool, and a tendril of acidic ooze-
[u5405 | 03:33:19.845-03:33:20.545 | Unknown] Yes, totally. I totally remember that
[u5406 | 03:33:20.545-03:33:21.085 | DM] Punched a hole in the-
[u5407 | 03:33:21.125-03:33:22.405 | Unknown] That's the last place where I was?
[u5408 | 03:33:22.505-03:33:23.585 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5409 | 03:33:23.645-03:33:25.165 | DM] That's when we went with the monks to try and-
[u5410 | 03:33:25.205-03:33:27.825 | Delwath] That is apparently where he was trying to heal the land.
[u5411 | 03:33:27.825-03:33:29.525 | Delwath] That's the seat of chaos. He was trying to get rid of it.
[u5412 | 03:33:29.525-03:33:31.285 | Seeker] So that was a portal or something.
[u5413 | 03:33:31.285-03:33:31.785 | Kenzo] Fuck.
[u5414 | 03:33:31.785-03:33:35.265 | Seeker] But, and all those monks basically died or didn't come back anyway.
[u5415 | 03:33:35.265-03:33:39.005 | DM] They either died or they got scattered around the planes when they were...
[u5416 | 03:33:40.145-03:33:41.345 | DM] when the chaos happened.
[u5417 | 03:33:47.765-03:33:49.845 | Wellby] What do you, what do you think the odds are of killing that dragon?
[u5418 | 03:33:55.245-03:33:55.885 | DM] I couldn't.
[u5419 | 03:33:56.785-03:33:57.285 | DM] I don't know.
[u5420 | 03:33:58.705-03:34:00.965 | DM] You're, like, not too shabby from what I've seen.
[u5421 | 03:34:04.125-03:34:06.985 | DM] I mean, you certainly avoided my trap neatly.
[u5422 | 03:34:06.985-03:34:12.485 | DM] [sighs] But, like...
[u5423 | 03:34:12.485-03:34:14.305 | Seeker] What would it take to lure her out of her lair, though?
[u5424 | 03:34:14.365-03:34:15.285 | Wellby] Yeah. What would get her out?
[u5425 | 03:34:15.305-03:34:16.585 | Kenzo] Yeah. We, we don't want to fight her in there.
[u5426 | 03:34:16.865-03:34:19.785 | Seeker] We've heard that she l-loves shiny things.
[u5427 | 03:34:21.205-03:34:21.625 | DM] Yeah.
[u5428 | 03:34:23.025-03:34:24.845 | Kenzo] But [coughs]...
[u5429 | 03:34:24.905-03:34:27.265 | Wellby] Why would she kill you if she knew you were on the island?
[u5430 | 03:34:29.425-03:34:32.005 | Wellby] 'Cause of your, like, escape attempt that you led?
[u5431 | 03:34:32.745-03:34:34.145 | Kenzo] Bad influence on the other dragonets.
[u5432 | 03:34:34.285-03:34:35.985 | DM] Yeah. She thinks I'm a bad influence.
[u5433 | 03:34:35.985-03:34:39.685 | Delwath] I mean, she might have a hatred of Rai who killed her father.
[u5434 | 03:34:40.485-03:34:41.125 | Wellby] Yeah, that's fair.
[u5435 | 03:34:43.145-03:34:49.045 | DM] I think she just likes her little simple empire here.
[u5436 | 03:34:50.505-03:34:53.045 | DM] She doesn't like anybody who kinda
[u5437 | 03:34:55.025-03:34:55.765 | DM] speaks up.
[u5438 | 03:34:56.385-03:34:57.445 | Unknown] So you're saying her, um,
[u5439 | 03:34:58.525-03:35:01.125 | Seeker] ambitions aren't as grandiose as Cha'mutte's.
[u5440 | 03:35:03.865-03:35:07.045 | DM] I see no reason why she couldn't leave this island if she wanted to.
[u5441 | 03:35:07.625-03:35:12.525 | Kenzo] So she's s- she's serving some kind of function here that's useful, of use to her deceased dad.
[u5442 | 03:35:16.005-03:35:19.385 | Kenzo] Um, if you were us and, uh,
[u5443 | 03:35:20.585-03:35:21.145 | Kenzo] you needed to
[u5444 | 03:35:22.445-03:35:23.945 | Kenzo] get that piece of jade back from the dragon,
[u5445 | 03:35:24.985-03:35:25.505 | Kenzo] any advice?
[u5446 | 03:35:31.585-03:35:32.745 | DM] If I were you?
[u5447 | 03:35:32.845-03:35:33.105 | Kenzo] Yeah.
[u5448 | 03:35:35.405-03:35:36.785 | DM] I'd pick an easier quest.
[u5449 | 03:35:37.745-03:35:38.325 | Kenzo] Eh.
[u5450 | 03:35:39.445-03:35:40.725 | Kenzo] Let's say you had to do this one, though.
[u5451 | 03:35:40.725-03:35:42.345 | DM] [laughs] Good.
[u5452 | 03:35:46.205-03:35:46.365 | DM] [laughs]
[u5453 | 03:35:46.505-03:35:47.625 | Unknown] Oh, Dimitar.
[u5454 | 03:35:47.985-03:35:48.525 | Kenzo] Does, um,
[u5455 | 03:35:50.025-03:35:51.645 | Delwath] she keep her deals for the most part?
[u5456 | 03:35:52.985-03:35:53.225 | DM] Yeah.
[u5457 | 03:35:56.705-03:35:57.445 | Kenzo] You think she would trade?
[u5458 | 03:35:58.985-03:36:03.965 | DM] She might. I mean, she might just, like, say, "I'll kill you and take your stuff anyway."
[u5459 | 03:36:04.965-03:36:06.165 | Kenzo] But that'd be fine with us.
[u5460 | 03:36:06.165-03:36:06.725 | Wellby] That's okay.
[u5461 | 03:36:06.725-03:36:08.585 | Delwath] Isn't that kind of the opposite of keeping your deals?
[u5462 | 03:36:10.725-03:36:18.245 | DM] Uh... [laughs]
[u5463 | 03:36:18.245-03:36:18.305 | Unknown] [laughs]
[u5464 | 03:36:18.405-03:36:19.305 | DM] She-
[u5465 | 03:36:19.305-03:36:20.885 | Unknown] When you said yes, did you mean no?
[u5466 | 03:36:20.885-03:36:21.385 | Kenzo] [laughs]
[u5467 | 03:36:21.385-03:36:22.005 | DM] No, no, no, no, no.
[u5468 | 03:36:22.385-03:36:22.445 | Kenzo] No.
[u5469 | 03:36:22.445-03:36:27.165 | DM] She's never, uh, she's never taken away a tower she gave to a dragonet.

[u5471 | 03:36:30.325-03:36:31.845 | DM] But, you know,
[u5472 | 03:36:34.725-03:36:40.665 | DM] who knows? Many of them might go in there and be like, "Hey, here's some stuff," and she says, "I don't like you, so I'll just take that stuff."

[u5489 | 03:37:32.305-03:37:37.225 | DM] [coughs] You guys, like, don't really do your research, do you?

[u5492 | 03:37:40.525-03:37:42.825 | Delwath] I... They said forever, which is clearly not true.

[u5495 | 03:37:48.825-03:37:50.845 | DM] were on Hkar with the humans.
[u5496 | 03:37:51.665-03:37:51.945 | Unknown] Oh.

[u5498 | 03:37:54.305-03:37:56.965 | Delwath] Didn't you just say you didn't realize there were so many of your people here?
